    29	#include <omp.h>
    30	
    31	#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
    32	
    33	// ============================================================================
    34	// Types and Constants
    35	// ============================================================================
    36	
    37	using idx_t = uint32_t;
    38	using val_t = float;
    39	
    40	constexpr val_t PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;
   380	            faces = std::move(newFaces);
   381	        }
   382	
   383	        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a "room")
   384	        triangles.reserve(faces.size());
   385	        for (const auto& face : faces) {
   386	            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward
   387	            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);
   388	        }
   389	    }
   390	};
   391	
   392	// ============================================================================
   393	// Random Number Generation
   394	// ============================================================================
   395	
   396	class RandomGenerator {
   397	    std::mt19937 rng;
   398	    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
   399	public:
   400	    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
   401	    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
   402	    void discard(uint64_t count) { rng.discard(count); }
   403	};
   404	
   405	// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates
   406	Vec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {
   407	    val_t u = rng.rand();
   408	    val_t v = rng.rand();
   409	    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
   410	        u = 1.0f - u;
   411	        v = 1.0f - v;
   412	    }
   413	    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;
   414	    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;
   415	    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;
   416	}
   417	
   418	// ============================================================================
   419	// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)
   420	// ============================================================================
   421	
   422	val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,
   423	                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {
   424	    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;
   425	    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;
   426	    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
   427	    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);
   428	
   429	    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   430	
   431	    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
   432	    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;
   433	    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
   434	    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   435	
   436	    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
   437	    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
   438	    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   439	
   440	    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;
   441	}
   442	
   443	// ============================================================================
   444	// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)
   445	// ============================================================================
   446	
   447	// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle
   448	// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search
   449	bool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to, const Vec3& dirNorm,
   450	                  val_t rayLen, const Octree& octree,
   451	                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {
   452	    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {
   453	        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;
   454	
   455	        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);
   456	        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {
   457	            return true;  // Ray is blocked
   458	        }
   459	        return false;
   460	    });
   461	}
   462	
   463	// ============================================================================
   464	// Form Factor Computation (Kij)
   465	// ============================================================================
   466	
   467	// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)
   468	val_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,
   469	                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,
   470	                  const Octree& octree,
   471	                  RandomGenerator& rng) {
   472	    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];
   473	    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];
   474	    const Vec3 normalI = triI.normal();
   475	    const Vec3 normalJ = triJ.normal();
   476	
   477	    // Cull triangles facing the same direction
   478	    if (normalI.dot(normalJ) > 0.99f) return ZERO;
   479	
   480	    val_t kij = ZERO;
   481	
   482	    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
   483	        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);
   484	        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);
   485	
   486	        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;
   487	        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();
   488	        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
   489	        val_t dist = std::sqrt(distSqr);
   490	
   491	        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, v / dist, dist,
   492	                         octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;
   493	
   494	        val_t cosPhiI = std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normalI) / dist);
   495	        val_t cosPhiJ = std::max(ZERO, (-v).dot(normalJ) / dist);
   496	
   497	        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;
   498	
   499	        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);
   500	    }
   501	
   502	    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;
   503	}
   504	
   505	// ============================================================================
   506	// Tau (time delay) Computation
   507	// ============================================================================
   508	
   509	int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
   510	    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
   511	    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
   512	}
   513	
   514	// ============================================================================
   515	// Room Response Simulation State
   520	    size_t numTimesteps;
   521	
   522	    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
   523	    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle
   524	    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)
   525	    std::unique_ptr<val_t[]> kij;   // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)
   526	    std::unique_ptr<int[]> tau;     // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)
   527	    std::unique_ptr<val_t[]> radE;  // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)
   528	    std::unique_ptr<val_t[]> radB;  // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)
   529	    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source
   530	
   531	    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure
   532	
   533	    size_t sourceIndex;
   534	
   535	    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
   536	    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
   537	};
   538	
   539	// ============================================================================
   540	// Initialization
   541	// ============================================================================
   542	
   543	void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
   544	                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
   545	    // Generate mesh
   546	    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
   547	    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
   548	    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
   549	    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
   550	    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
   551	
   552	    printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
   553	
   554	    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
   555	    printf("Building octree...\n");
   556	    state.octree.build(state.triangles);
   557	
   558	    // Initialize areas
   559	    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
   560	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)
   561	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   562	        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
   563	    }
   564	
   565	    // Initialize reflectivity
   566	    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);
   567	
   568	    // Initialize matrices
   569	    const size_t matrixSize = state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles;
   570	    const size_t radiositySize = timesteps * state.numTriangles;
   571	    // Default-initialization deliberately leaves scalar arrays untouched. The
   572	    // parallel loops below establish NUMA-local first-touch placement.
   573	    state.kij.reset(new val_t[matrixSize]);
   574	    state.tau.reset(new int[matrixSize]);
   575	    state.radE.reset(new val_t[radiositySize]);
   576	    state.radB.reset(new val_t[radiositySize]);
   577	    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   578	
   579	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)
   580	    for (size_t i = 0; i < radiositySize; ++i) {
   581	        state.radE[i] = ZERO;
   582	        state.radB[i] = ZERO;
   583	    }
   584	
   585	    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
   586	    size_t timeOn = 0;
   587	    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
   588	    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
   589	        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
   590	    }
   591	}
   592	
   593	// ============================================================================
   594	// Precomputation Phase
   595	// ============================================================================
   596	
   597	void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
   598	    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
   599	    size_t completed = 0;
   600	
   601	    // Preserve the exact random sequence of the original row-major loop. A
   602	    // form-factor call consumes four random values per ray unless it is
   603	    // rejected by the normal cull before sampling. Capturing the generator at
   604	    // each row boundary lets the expensive geometry work run independently.
   605	    std::vector<RandomGenerator> rowGenerators;
   606	    rowGenerators.reserve(state.numTriangles);
   607	    RandomGenerator serialGenerator(42);
   608	    constexpr uint64_t drawsPerPair = 4u * NUM_RAYS;
   609	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   610	        rowGenerators.push_back(serialGenerator);
   611	        const Vec3 normalI = state.triangles[i].normal();
   612	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   613	            if (i != j && normalI.dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
   614	                serialGenerator.discard(drawsPerPair);
   615	            }
   616	        }
   617	    }
   618	
   619	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)
   620	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   621	        RandomGenerator rng = rowGenerators[i];
   622	        const size_t row = i * state.numTriangles;
   623	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   624	            state.kij[row + j] = (i == j)
   625	                ? ZERO
   626	                : computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
   627	        }
   628	
   629	        size_t done;
   630	        #pragma omp atomic capture
   631	        done = ++completed;
   632	        if (done % 100 == 0 || done == state.numTriangles) {
   633	            #pragma omp critical(roomsim_progress)
   634	            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", done, state.numTriangles);
   635	        }
   636	    }
   637	}
   638	
   639	void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
   640	    printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");
   641	
   642	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)
   643	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   644	        const size_t row = i * state.numTriangles;
   645	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   646	            state.tau[row + j] = (i == j)
   647	                ? 0
   648	                : computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
   649	        }
   650	    }
   651	}
   652	
   653	// ============================================================================
   654	// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
   655	// ============================================================================
   656	
   657	void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
   658	    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
   659	
   660	    // Timesteps are dependent, but all receiver triangles within a timestep
   661	    // are independent because every non-diagonal delay is at least one.
   662	    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)
   663	    {
   664	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   665	            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
   666	            for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   667	                val_t sumB = ZERO;
   668	                const size_t row = i * state.numTriangles;
   669	
   670	                for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   671	                    if (i == j) continue;
   672	
   673	                    int tauij = state.tau[row + j];
   674	
   675	                    // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
   676	                    if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;
   677	
   678	                    val_t kij = state.kij[row + j];
   679	                    if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
   680	
   681	                    // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
   682	                    size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
   683	                    val_t radJ = state.radB[srcTime * state.numTriangles + j];
   684	                    if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
   685	
   686	                    // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
   687	                    sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
   688	                }
   689	
   690	                // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
   691	                const size_t output = t * state.numTriangles + i;
   692	                state.radB[output] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[output];
   693	            }
   694	
   695	            #pragma omp single nowait
   696	            if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
   697	                printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
   698	            }
   699	        }
   700	    }
   701	}
   702	
   703	// ============================================================================
   704	// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
   705	// ============================================================================
   706	
   707	void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
   708	    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
   709	
   710	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)
   711	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   712	        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
   713	        int bestT = 0;
   714	
   715	        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
   716	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   717	            val_t sum = ZERO;
   718	
   719	            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
   720	                val_t pB = state.radB[tt * state.numTriangles + i];
   721	                val_t pS = state.radB[(tt - t) * state.numTriangles + state.sourceIndex];
   722	                sum += pS * pB;
   723	            }
   724	
   725	            if (sum > maxCorr) {
   726	                maxCorr = sum;
   727	                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
   728	            }
   729	        }
   730	
   731	        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
   732	    }
   733	}
   734	
   735	// ============================================================================
   850	    printf("Options:\n");
   851	    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
   852	    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
   853	    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
   854	    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
   855	    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
   856	    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
   857	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   858	    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
   859	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   860	}
   861	
   862	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   863	    // Do not let an OpenMP runtime silently serialize or shrink this benchmark.
   864	    omp_set_dynamic(0);
   865	
   866	    int targetTriangles = 320;
   867	    int timesteps = 50;
   868	    int sourceIdx = 0;
   869	    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
   870	    bool validate = false;
   871	    bool printResults = false;
   872	
   873	    // Parse command line arguments
   874	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   875	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   876	            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
   877	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   878	            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
   879	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   880	            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
   881	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   882	            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
   883	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   884	            validate = true;
   885	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
   886	            printResults = true;
   887	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   888	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   889	            return 0;
   890	        } else {
   891	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   892	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   893	            return 1;
   894	        }
   895	    }
   896	
   897	    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);
   898	
   899	    printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
   900	    printf("===================================\n");
   901	    printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
   902	    printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
   903	    printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
   904	    printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
   905	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   906	    printf("OpenMP threads: %d\n", omp_get_max_threads());
   907	    printf("\n");
   908	
   909	    // Initialize
   910	    SimulationState state;
   911	    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
   912	                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);
   913	
   914	    printf("\n");
   915	
   916	    // Precomputation
   917	    auto startPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   918	
   919	    computeTimeDelays(state);
   920	    computeFormFactors(state);
   921	
   922	    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   923	    auto preDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();
   924	
   925	    printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);

codex
Implemented OpenMP parallelization while preserving deterministic, equivalent results.

Key changes:

- Parallelized form factors, time delays, per-timestep receiver updates, initialization, and distance correlation.
- Preserved the original random-number sequence across thread counts.
- Added NUMA-friendly parallel first-touch allocation and static scheduling.
- Reused one persistent parallel region during wave propagation.
- Made OpenMP mandatory through CMake; executable remains `roomsim`.
- No files were added.

Files changed:

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_omp_r5/roomsim/roomsim.cpp:597)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_omp_r5/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt:1)

Validation passed with identical hashes across tested thread counts and alternate inputs. ASan/UBSan also passed.

Observed 1,280-triangle performance on the available 128-core system:

- 1 thread: 26.046 s
- 8 threads: 3.316 s
- 32 threads: 0.891 s
- 128 threads: 0.263 s

That is approximately 99× speedup at 128 threads.

Implemented OpenMP parallelization while preserving deterministic, equivalent results.

Key changes:

- Parallelized form factors, time delays, per-timestep receiver updates, initialization, and distance correlation.
- Preserved the original random-number sequence across thread counts.
- Added NUMA-friendly parallel first-touch allocation and static scheduling.
- Reused one persistent parallel region during wave propagation.
- Made OpenMP mandatory through CMake; executable remains `roomsim`.
- No files were added.

Validation passed with identical hashes across tested thread counts and alternate inputs. ASan/UBSan also passed.

Observed 1,280-triangle performance on the available 128-core system:

- 1 thread: 26.046 s
- 8 threads: 3.316 s
- 32 threads: 0.891 s
- 128 threads: 0.263 s

That is approximately 99× speedup at 128 threads.
